Large language models (LLMs) now offer the promise of custom-defined feeds--but users often fail to foresee the gaps and edge cases in how they define their custom feed. We introduce feed elicitation interviews, an interactive method that guides users through identifying these gaps and articulating their preferences to better author custom social media feeds. We deploy this approach in an online study to create custom BlueSky feeds and find that participants significantly prefer the feeds produced from their elicited preferences to those produced by users manually describing their feeds. Through feed elicitation interviews, we advance users' ability to control their social media experience, empowering them to describe and implement their desired feeds.

</details>


### [385] [One Year After the PDPL: a Glimpse into the E-Commerce World in Saudi Arabia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18616)
*Eman Alashwali,Abeer Alhuzali*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究评估沙特阿拉伯100个电商网站对《个人数据保护法》(PDPL)的合规情况，发现仅31%网站隐私政策完整声明四项关键要求，且高排名网站合规性更差，同时探索了使用大语言模型自动分析隐私政策的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沙特阿拉伯《个人数据保护法》(PDPL)于2024年生效，但缺乏对其实际实施情况的评估。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过分析电商网站的隐私政策合规性，了解数据保护法在非西方背景下的实施障碍。

Method: 研究分析了沙特阿拉伯100个电商网站，检查其隐私政策是否包含四项关键声明：a)个人数据保留期限，b)请求销毁个人数据的权利，c)请求获取个人数据副本的权利，d)投诉机制。同时评估了基于搜索排名和托管平台的合规差异，并探索使用大语言模型(LLMs)作为隐私政策自动分析工具的可行性。

Result: 1) 仅31%的网站隐私政策完整声明了四项要求；2) 即使包含声明，许多也缺乏必要的细节；3) 高排名电商网站和本地电商托管平台上的网站不合规率显著更高；4) 大语言模型在隐私政策自动分析方面显示出潜力，但需要改进。

Conclusion: 尽管有国家层面的意识和支持，沙特电商网站对PDPL的合规性仍然不足，特别是在高流量网站中。研究为理解非西方背景下数据保护法的实施障碍提供了重要见解，建议政策制定者、监管机构、网站所有者和开发者采取措施改进数据保护实践，并探索使用大语言模型等工具自动化合规监控。

Abstract: In 2024, Saudi Arabia's Personal Data Protection Law (PDPL) came into force. However, little work has been done to assess its implementation. In this paper, we analyzed 100 e-commerce websites in Saudi Arabia against the PDPL, examining the presence of a privacy policy and, if present, the policy's declarations of four items pertaining to personal data rights and practices: a) personal data retention period, b) the right to request the destruction of personal data, c) the right to request a copy of personal data, and d) a mechanism for filing complaints. Our results show that, despite national awareness and support efforts, a significant fraction of e-commerce websites in our dataset are not fully compliant: only 31% of the websites in our dataset declared all four examined items in their privacy policies. Even when privacy policies included such declarations, a considerable fraction of them failed to cover required fine-grained details. Second, the majority of top-ranked e-commerce websites (based on search results order) and those hosted on local e-commerce hosting platforms exhibited considerably higher non-compliance rates than mid- to low-ranked websites and those not hosted on e-commerce platforms. Third, we assessed the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) as an automated tool for privacy policy analysis to measure compliance with the PDPL. We highlight the potential of LLMs and suggest considerations to improve LLM-based automated analysis for privacy policies. Our results provide a step forward in understanding the implementation barriers to data protection laws, especially in non-Western contexts. We provide recommendations for policymakers, regulators, website owners, and developers seeking to improve data protection practices and automate compliance monitoring.

</details>


### [386] [Finding the Signal in the Noise: An Exploratory Study on Assessing the Effectiveness of AI and Accessibility Forums for Blind Users' Support Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18623)
*Satwik Ram Kodandaram,Jiawei Zhou,Xiaojun Bi,IV Ramakrishnan,Vikas Ashok*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 盲人用户使用论坛和生成式AI工具获取计算机交互解决方案时面临认知负荷过高和可靠性不足的问题，需要改进设计以提供更可信、认知可管理的支持


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解盲人用户在使用论坛和生成式AI工具获取计算机交互解决方案、辅助技术信息、屏幕阅读器功能、教程和软件更新时的体验，以识别和解决持续存在的支持缺口

Method: 对14名经常使用论坛和生成式AI工具的盲人用户进行访谈研究

Result: 论坛存在多个重叠主题、冗余或不相关内容、需要用户自行整合的碎片化响应，增加了认知负荷；生成式AI工具虽然提供更直接的帮助，但存在不可靠答案、过度冗长或碎片化的指导、捏造信息、不遵循提示的矛盾建议等问题，增加了验证需求

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出了改进辅助资源可靠性的设计机会，旨在为盲人用户提供更可信和认知可管理的支持

Abstract: Accessibility forums and, more recently, generative AI tools have become vital resources for blind users seeking solutions to computer-interaction issues and learning about new assistive technologies, screen reader features, tutorials, and software updates. Understanding user experiences with these resources is essential for identifying and addressing persistent support gaps. Towards this, we interviewed 14 blind users who regularly engage with forums and GenAI tools. Findings revealed that forums often overwhelm users with multiple overlapping topics, redundant or irrelevant content, and fragmented responses that must be mentally pieced together, increasing cognitive load. GenAI tools, while offering more direct assistance, introduce new barriers by producing unreliable answers, including overly verbose or fragmented guidance, fabricated information, and contradictory suggestions that fail to follow prompts, thereby heightening verification demands. Based on these insights, we outlined design opportunities to improve the reliability of assistive resources, aiming to provide blind users with more trustworthy and cognitively-manageable support.

</details>


### [387] [Lost in Instructions: Study of Blind Users' Experiences with DIY Manuals and AI-Rewritten Instructions for Assembly, Operation, and Troubleshooting of Tangible Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18630)
*Monalika Padma Reddy,Aruna Balasubramanian,Jiawei Zhou,Xiaojun Bi,IV Ramakrishnan,Vikas Ashok*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 盲人使用AI工具和产品手册进行DIY任务时面临挑战，AI工具目前未能有效解决产品手册对盲人用户不友好的问题，反而可能提供不完整、不连贯或误导性的指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI工具如ChatGPT和Be-My-AI被盲人越来越多地使用，但关于他们如何利用这些工具和产品手册资源来组装、操作和排除物理或有形产品故障的研究很少，这些任务需要空间推理、结构理解和精确执行。

Method: 通过访谈研究和可用性研究，调查盲人参与者如何利用AI工具和产品手册进行物理产品的DIY任务。

Result: 研究发现：1）产品手册是重要资源，但对盲人用户来说往往不够充分；2）AI工具目前未能充分解决这一问题，反而经常提供不完整、不连贯或误导性的指导，加剧了问题；3）产品手册指令对盲人用户通常不适用。

Conclusion: 需要改进AI工具，使其能够为盲人用户涉及有形产品的DIY任务生成量身定制的指令。

Abstract: AI tools like ChatGPT and Be-My-AI are increasingly being used by blind individuals. Although prior work has explored their use in some Do-It-Yourself (DIY) tasks by blind individuals, little is known about how they use these tools and the available product-manual resources to assemble, operate, and troubleshoot physical or tangible products - tasks requiring spatial reasoning, structural understanding, and precise execution. We address this knowledge gap via an interview study and a usability study with blind participants, investigating how they leverage AI tools and product manuals for DIY tasks with physical products. Findings show that manuals are essential resources, but product-manual instructions are often inadequate for blind users. AI tools presently do not adequately address this insufficiency; in fact, we observed that they often exacerbate this issue with incomplete, incoherent, or misleading guidance. Lastly, we suggest improvements to AI tools for generating tailored instructions for blind users' DIY tasks involving tangible products.

</details>


### [388] [Searching Through Complex Worlds: Visual Search and Spatial Regularity Memory in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18669)
*Lefan Lai,Tinghui Li,Zhanna Sarsenbayeva,Brandon Victor Syiem*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了混合现实环境中视觉搜索性能的影响因素，发现次要听觉任务对搜索性能无显著影响但增加工作负荷，复杂环境和虚拟元素深度显著阻碍搜索，参与者对重复空间配置表现出隐性记忆而非显性识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉搜索是混合现实交互的核心组件，受MR应用场景复杂性的影响。研究旨在探究MR中普遍存在的因素如何影响视觉搜索性能和空间规律记忆，为不同应用场景的MR设计提供关键考虑。

Method: 研究通过实验方法考察了物理环境复杂性、次要任务存在、虚拟内容深度和空间布局配置等因素对视觉搜索的影响。使用感知工作负荷测量和性能指标评估这些因素的作用。

Result: 次要听觉任务对视觉搜索性能无显著主效应，但显著提高了所有条件下的感知工作负荷。复杂环境和变化的虚拟元素深度显著阻碍视觉搜索，但未显著增加感知工作负荷。参与者未能显性识别虚拟元素的重复空间配置，但在搜索重复配置时表现显著更好，表明存在空间规律的隐性记忆。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于MR视觉搜索的新见解，强调了在设计不同应用场景的MR系统时需要考虑的关键因素，包括环境复杂性、任务负荷和空间布局对用户性能的影响。

Abstract: Visual search is a core component of mixed reality (MR) interactions, influenced by the complexities of MR application contexts. In this paper, we investigate how prevalent factors in MR influence visual search performance and spatial regularity memory -- including the physical environment complexity, secondary task presence, virtual content depth and spatial layout configurations. Contrary to prior work, we found that the secondary auditory task did not have a significant main effect on visual search performance, while significantly elevating higher perceived workload measures in all conditions. Complex environments and varied virtual elements depths significantly hinder visual search, but did not significantly increase perceived workload measures. Finally, participants did not explicitly recognize repeated spatial configurations of virtual elements, but performed significantly better when searching repeated spatial configurations, suggesting implicit memory of spatial regularities. Our work presents novel insights on visual search and highlights key considerations when designing MR for different application contexts.

</details>


### [389] [MagHeart: Exploring Playful Avatar Co-Creation and Shared Heartbeats for Icebreaking in Hybrid Meetings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18676)
*Black Sun,Haiyang Xu,Ge Kacy Fu,Liyue Da,Eve Hoggan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MagHeart是一个多模态系统，通过乐高化身共创和心跳磁力设备，探索混合会议中的对称破冰，改善远程参与者的社交存在感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合会议通常以社交尴尬和参与不对称开始，特别是远程参与者缺乏面对面的非正式互动机会，导致他们难以融入会议开场阶段。

Method: 开发了MagHeart系统，结合乐高化身共创游戏和可触摸的磁力心跳设备，通过创造性协作和抽象生物反馈，使远程参与者获得物质和感知上的存在感。采用基于场景的探索性研究，结合定量和定性数据，从共处和远程两个角度评估参与者的预期参与度、感知社交存在感和未来使用意愿。

Result: 研究结果表明，游戏化、具身化的破冰活动有助于支持早期混合互动，同时也揭示了隐私、分心和情境适当性等方面的紧张关系。参与者对系统的社交存在感和参与度有积极反馈。

Conclusion: 这项工作为未来混合会议工具提供了设计见解和开放性问题，需要在游戏性、具身性和社交敏感性之间取得平衡，以创造更包容的混合会议体验。

Abstract: Hybrid meetings often begin with social awkwardness and asymmetric participation, particularly for remote attendees who lack access to informal, co-present interaction. We present MagHeart, a multimodal system that explores symmetric icebreaking in hybrid meetings through playful LEGO-based avatar co-creation and a tangible magnetic device that represents a remote participant's heartbeat as an ambient presence cue. By combining creative co-creation with abstract bio-feedback, MagHeart rethinks how remote participants can become materially and perceptually present during meeting openings. We report findings from a scenario-based exploratory study combining quantitative and qualitative data, examining participants' anticipated engagement, perceived social presence, and future-use intentions from both co-located and remote perspectives. Our results highlight opportunities for playful, embodied icebreakers to support early hybrid interaction, while also surfacing tensions around privacy, distraction, and contextual appropriateness. This work contributes design insights and open questions for future hybrid meeting tools that balance playfulness, embodiment, and social sensitivity.

</details>


### [390] [See What I See: An Attention-Guiding eHMI Approach for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18798)
*Jialong Li,Zhenyu Mao,Zhiyao Wang,Yijun Lu,Shogo Morita,Nianyu Li,Kenji Tei*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AGeHMI：一种基于投影的外部人机界面，通过方向提示和风险颜色编码主动引导行人注意力至环境危险，而非仅显示自动驾驶车辆自身状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有eHMI设计仅关注自动驾驶车辆自身状态，可能无意中吸引行人注意力或导致行人过度依赖AV信号，忽视扫描周围其他危险，需要改进以增强车辆-行人通信安全性。

Method: 提出注意力引导eHMI（AGeHMI），采用基于投影的可视化技术，结合方向提示和基于风险的颜色编码，主动引导行人注意力至潜在环境危险。

Result: 虚拟现实用户研究（N=20）表明，AGeHMI能有效影响参与者视觉注意力分布，显著降低与周围车辆的潜在碰撞风险，同时提升主观信心并减少认知负荷。

Conclusion: AGeHMI通过主动引导行人注意力至环境危险而非仅显示车辆状态，为增强自动驾驶车辆与行人通信提供了更安全有效的解决方案。

Abstract: As autonomous vehicles are gradually being deployed in the real world, external Human-Machine Interfaces (eHMIs) are expected to serve as a critical solution for enhancing vehicle-pedestrian communication. However, existing eHMI designs typically focus solely on the ego vehicle's status, which can inadvertently capture pedestrians' attention or encourage misguided reliance on the AV's signals, leading them to neglect scanning for other surrounding hazards. To address this, we propose the Attention-Guiding eHMI (AGeHMI), a projection-based visualization that employs directional cues and risk-based color coding to actively guide pedestrians' attention toward potential environmental dangers. Evaluation through a virtual reality user study (N = 20) suggests that AGeHMI effectively influences participants' visual attention distribution and significantly reduces potential collision risks with surrounding vehicles, while simultaneously improving subjective confidence and reducing cognitive workload.

</details>


### [391] [Chat-Based Support Alone May Not Be Enough: Comparing Conversational and Embedded LLM Feedback for Mathematical Proof Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18807)
*Eason Chen,Sophia Judicke,Kayla Beigh,Xinyi Tang,Isabel Wang,Nina Yuan,Zimo Xiao,Chuangji Li,Shizhuo Li,Reed Luttmer,Shreya Singh,Maria Yampolsky,Naman Parikh,Yvonne Zhao,Meiyi Chen,Scarlett Huang,Anishka Mohanty,Gregory Johnson,John Mackey,Jionghao Lin,Ken Koedinger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 评估GPTutor——一个面向本科离散数学课程的LLM驱动辅导系统，包含结构化证明评审工具和数学问题聊天机器人。实验显示早期使用能提高作业成绩，但未转化为考试成绩提升。聊天机器人使用频率高且寻求答案行为与后续考试成绩负相关，而证明评审工具则无此负面关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM驱动的辅导系统在数学证明学习中的实际效果，特别是比较结构化反馈工具与开放式聊天机器人在支持学习迁移方面的差异，了解不同工具使用模式对学生学习成果的影响。

Method: 采用交错访问研究设计，148名本科生参与。系统包含两个组件：结构化证明评审工具（提供嵌入式反馈）和数学问题聊天机器人。通过使用日志、人工编码和自动分类器分析学生行为模式，控制先前表现和自我效能感等因素，评估工具使用与学习成果的关系。

Result: 早期访问GPTutor与实验期间作业成绩提高相关，但未观察到考试成绩的迁移效应。低自我效能感和先前考试成绩较差的学生更频繁使用两个组件。聊天机器人使用频率高和寻求答案行为与后续期中考试成绩负相关，而证明评审工具使用则无独立关联。

Conclusion: 仅依赖聊天机器人支持可能无法可靠促进数学证明学习成果向独立评估的迁移，而基于作业的结构化反馈工具则较少与学习效果降低相关。这表明在LLM教育应用中，工具设计和交互结构对学习效果有重要影响。

Abstract: We evaluate GPTutor, an LLM-powered tutoring system for an undergraduate discrete mathematics course. It integrates two LLM-supported tools: a structured proof-review tool that provides embedded feedback on students' written proof attempts, and a chatbot for math questions. In a staggered-access study with 148 students, earlier access was associated with higher homework performance during the interval when only the experimental group could use the system, while we did not observe this performance increase transfer to exam scores. Usage logs show that students with lower self-efficacy and prior exam performance used both components more frequently. Session-level behavioral labels, produced by human coding and scaled using an automated classifier, characterize how students engaged with the chatbot (e.g., answer-seeking or help-seeking). In models controlling for prior performance and self-efficacy, higher chatbot usage and answer-seeking behavior were negatively associated with subsequent midterm performance, whereas proof-review usage showed no detectable independent association. Together, the findings suggest that chatbot-based support alone may not reliably support transfer to independent assessment of math proof-learning outcomes, whereas work-anchored, structured feedback appears less associated with reduced learning.

</details>


### [392] [When Friction Helps: Transaction Confirmation Improves Decision Quality in Blockchain Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18834)
*Eason Chen,Xinyi Tang,George Digkas,Dionysios Lougaris,John E. Naulty,Kostas Chalkias*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 区块链交易确认可能不是单纯的可用性摩擦，而是具有认知意义的检查点，能在不可逆操作前提供自我纠正机会


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究区块链应用中交易确认的功能性作用，探索其作为决策边界（深思熟虑与不可逆承诺之间）的价值，而非仅仅视为可用性摩擦

Method: 使用基于区块链的Connect Four游戏进行实验，设计两种交互模式：手动钱包确认模式（确认模式）与自动授权委托模式（无摩擦模式），通过对比研究确认对决策质量的影响

Result: 尽管参与者偏好无摩擦模式并认为性能更好，但客观表现更差（胜率降低11.8%，移动质量下降0.051）。取消提交的分析表明确认能实现提交前的自我纠正

Conclusion: 交易确认可作为认知上有意义的检查点，在不可逆区块链交互中存在交互流畅性与决策质量之间的权衡，确认机制能提升决策质量

Abstract: In blockchain applications, transaction confirmation is often treated as usability friction to be minimized or removed. However, confirmation also marks the boundary between deliberation and irreversible commitment, suggesting it may play a functional role in human decision-making. To investigate this tension, we conducted an experiment using a blockchain-based Connect Four game with two interaction modes differing only in authorization flow: manual wallet confirmation (Confirmation Mode) versus auto-authorized delegation (Frictionless Mode). Although participants preferred Frictionless Mode and perceived better performance (N=109), objective performance was worse without confirmation in a counterbalanced deployment (Wave 2: win rate -11.8%, p=0.044; move quality -0.051, p=0.022). Analysis of canceled submissions suggests confirmation can enable pre-submission self-correction (N=66, p=0.005). These findings suggest that transaction confirmation can function as a cognitively meaningful checkpoint rather than mere usability friction, highlighting a trade-off between interaction smoothness and decision quality in irreversible blockchain interactions.

</details>


### [393] [NeuroWise: A Multi-Agent LLM "Glass-Box" System for Practicing Double-Empathy Communication with Autistic Partners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18962)
*Albert Tang,Yifan Mo,Jie Li,Yue Su,Mengyuan Zhang,Sander L. Koole,Koen Hindriks,Jiahuan Pei*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: NeuroWise是一个基于多智能体LLM的辅导系统，帮助神经典型用户通过压力可视化、内部体验解释和情境指导来改善与神经多样性个体的沟通，减少缺陷归因框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双共情问题指出神经多样性和神经典型个体之间的沟通困难源于相互误解，但现有干预大多只关注自闭症个体。需要开发支持神经典型用户理解沟通挑战的工具。

Method: 开发NeuroWise多智能体LLM辅导系统，提供压力可视化、内部体验解释和情境指导。采用被试间设计研究(N=30)，比较NeuroWise用户与基线用户在困难互动后的归因变化和沟通效率。

Result: 所有参与者都认为NeuroWise有帮助。NeuroWise用户在缺陷归因方面有显著条件-时间效应(p=0.02)：减少缺陷框架，而基线用户转向责怪自闭症"缺陷"。NeuroWise用户沟通效率更高(减少37%轮次，p=0.03)。

Conclusion: 基于AI的解释可以支持归因改变，帮助用户认识到沟通挑战是相互的，而不仅仅是神经多样性个体的"缺陷"。NeuroWise为改善神经多样性和神经典型个体之间的沟通提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The double empathy problem frames communication difficulties between neurodivergent and neurotypical individuals as arising from mutual misunderstanding, yet most interventions focus on autistic individuals. We present NeuroWise, a multi-agent LLM-based coaching system that supports neurotypical users through stress visualization, interpretation of internal experiences, and contextual guidance. In a between-subjects study (N=30), NeuroWise was rated as helpful by all participants and showed a significant condition-time effect on deficit-based attributions (p=0.02): NeuroWise users reduced deficit framing, while baseline users shifted toward blaming autistic "deficits" after difficult interactions. NeuroWise users also completed conversations more efficiently (37% fewer turns, p=0.03). These findings suggest that AI-based interpretation can support attributional change by helping users recognize communication challenges as mutual.

</details>


### [394] [Miniaturized Pneumatic Actuator Array for Multipoint Deep Pressure Tactile Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18992)
*Ava Chen,Megan C. Coram,Cosima du Pasquier,Allison M. Okamura*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于热塑性涂层纺织品的制造方法，可一次性热压成型多个气动囊袋执行器阵列，无需牺牲层，适用于指尖可穿戴设备，能产生超过2N的力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴分布式触觉设备难以在小尺寸下提供足够力量（>1N）以引发深度压力感，同时保持最小负担。需要一种能产生足够力且制造简单的小型化触觉执行器阵列。

Method: 采用热塑性涂层纺织品制造气动执行器阵列。通过将气动入口引导到织物的共同折叠线，可在单一简单热压操作中形成多个气动囊袋执行器，无需牺牲阻挡层。该方法支持多种执行器直径和间距，最小可达8mm直径、1mm间距。

Result: 制造出紧凑的纺织气动执行器阵列（8mm直径，1mm间距）。在阻塞力测试中，小型气动纺织执行器在230kPa压力下可施加2.1N的力。通过一对执行器展示了能产生区分性和叠加性刺激的应用，表明可通过纺织执行器阵列调节振幅的恰可察觉差异。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了小型化、高力输出的纺织气动执行器阵列制造，为指尖可穿戴触觉设备提供了可行方案，能够产生深度压力感并调节刺激强度。

Abstract: Wearable distributed tactile devices aim to provide multipoint touch stimuli, but struggle to provide sufficient forces (> 1 N) at frequencies to invoke deep pressure sensation with minimal encumbrance at small scales. This work presents a method of fabricating arrays of pneumatic actuators from thermoplastic-coated textiles. By routing pneumatic inlets to a common fold line in the fabric, we demonstrate that multiple pneumatic pouch actuators can be formed in a single simple heat-pressing operation that does not require the use of sacrificial blocking layers. The method accommodates a range of actuator diameters and spacing distances, including as compact as 8 mm diameter actuators spaced 1 mm apart, which enables use in fingertip wearable devices. In a blocked force test, these small pneumatic textile actuators exert 2.1 N when pressurized to 230 kPa. With this pair of actuators, we demonstrate an example application in which we invoke both distinct and summative stimuli, suggesting the possibility of titrating just noticeable difference in amplitude with a textile actuator array.

</details>


### [395] [Who Has the Final Word? Designing Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for Professional Translators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19016)
*George X. Wang,Jiaqian Hu,Jing Qian*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CHORUS是一个基于MQM框架的人类-AI多智能体协作翻译系统，通过分解质量维度为专门智能体，在译者控制下进行迭代优化，优于零次学习和单智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM翻译技术取得进展，但在高风险领域仍需专业译者参与，现有工具多为单次生成或单智能体自优化，无法充分支持译者多维决策过程，缺乏对译者输入的结构化利用。

Method: 提出CHORUS框架，基于多维质量指标(MQM)框架，将质量维度分解为专门智能体，并将它们的反馈整合到由译者控制的迭代优化循环中，实现人类-AI多智能体协作翻译。

Result: 六名专业译者的初步研究发现，CHORUS在性能上持续优于零次学习和单智能体基线，表明MQM对齐的多智能体协作比自主生成更好地支持专业翻译工作流程。

Conclusion: 基于MQM的多智能体协作框架CHORUS能够有效支持专业翻译工作流程，通过分解质量维度和迭代优化，在保持译者控制权的同时提升翻译质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM based translation have led to renewed interest in fully automated systems, yet professional translators remain essential in high stakes domains where decisions about accuracy, terminology, style, and audience cannot be safely automated. Current tools are typically single shot generators or single-agent self-refiners, offering limited support for translator multidimensional decision making process and providing little structured leverage for translator input. We present CHORUS, a human-AI multiagent collaborative translation framework grounded in the Multidimensional Quality Metrics (MQM) framework, which decomposes quality dimensions into specialized agents and integrates their feedback into an iterative refinement loop controlled by the translator. A six-user preliminary study with professional translators found that CHORUS consistently outperforms zero-shot and single-agent baselines, showing that MQM-aligned multi-agent collaboration better supports professional translation workflows than autonomous generation.

</details>


### [396] [To Slide or Not to Slide: Exploring Techniques for Comparing Immersive Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19048)
*Xizi Wang,Yue Lyu,Yalong Yang,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究将2D媒体中的"滑动"概念与切换、并排两种比较策略结合，开发了五种沉浸式视频比较技术，并通过用户研究评估其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式视频(IVs)在360°环境中分布信息，使得比较变得认知负担重且高度依赖交互技术。随着IVs的普及，有效的比较技术已成为重要但尚未充分探索的研究领域。

Method: 受2D媒体中"滑动"概念的启发，整合切换和并排两种比较策略，在VR和2D环境中实现五种IV比较技术：SlideInVR、ToggleInVR、SlideIn2D、ToggleIn2D和SideBySideIn2D，并进行用户研究(N=20)评估。

Result: 研究发现SlideInVR和SlideIn2D被参与者一致评为最灵活和最受欢迎的IV比较方法。研究提供了每种技术优缺点的实证见解，强调在不同场景中切换比较方法的必要性。

Conclusion: 滑动式比较技术在沉浸式视频比较中表现出优越的灵活性和用户偏好，为IV比较技术设计提供了实证基础，并揭示了在不同场景中采用多种比较策略的重要性。

Abstract: Immersive videos (IVs) provide 360° environments that create a strong sense of presence and spatial exploration. Unlike traditional videos, IVs distribute information across multiple directions, making comparison cognitively demanding and highly dependent on interaction techniques. With the growing adoption of IVs, effective comparison techniques have become an essential yet underexplored area of research. Inspired by the "sliding" concept in 2D media comparison, we integrate two established comparison strategies from the literature--toggle and side-by-side--to support IV comparison with greater flexibility. For an in-depth understanding of different strategies, we adapt and implement five IV comparison techniques across VR and 2D environments: SlideInVR, ToggleInVR, SlideIn2D, ToggleIn2D, and SideBySideIn2D. We then conduct a user study (N=20) to examine how these techniques shape users' perceptions, strategies, and workflows. Our findings provide empirical insights into the strengths and limitations of each technique, underscoring the need to switch between comparison approaches across scenarios. Notably, participants consistently rate SlideInVR and SlideIn2D as the most flexible and favorite methods for IV comparison.

</details>


### [397] [Dark and Bright Side of Participatory Red-Teaming with Targets of Stereotyping for Eliciting Harmful Behaviors from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19124)
*Sieun Kim,Yeeun Jo,Sungmin Na,Hyunseung Lim,Eunchae Lee,Yu Min Choi,Soohyun Cho,Hwajung Hong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了让受刻板印象影响的群体参与AI红队测试，以发现语言模型中的偏见，同时关注参与者的心理福祉和赋权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红队测试通过制作对抗性提示来暴露AI的有害行为和评估风险，是发现语言模型中潜在刻板偏见的有效方法。由于这些微妙的伤害最好由有亲身经历的人识别，让受刻板印象影响的群体参与红队测试至关重要。然而，在利用他们的生活经验进行红队测试的同时保障其心理健康仍面临重大挑战。

Method: 研究采用混合方法分析，对20名在韩国因非名校毕业生刻板印象而受污名化的个体进行了参与式红队测试的实证研究。

Result: 研究发现参与者将亲身经历的歧视转化为识别偏见的战略专长，但同时面临心理成本，如压力和对其群体身份的负面反思。值得注意的是，红队参与增强了他们的能动性和赋权感，因为他们扮演了AI生态系统守护者的角色。

Conclusion: 研究讨论了设计参与式红队测试的启示，强调既要优先考虑对受污名化群体的伦理对待，又要促进他们的赋权。这需要在利用生活经验识别AI偏见与保障参与者心理健康之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Red-teaming, where adversarial prompts are crafted to expose harmful behaviors and assess risks, offers a dynamic approach to surfacing underlying stereotypical bias in large language models. Because such subtle harms are best recognized by those with lived experience, involving targets of stereotyping as red-teamers is essential. However, critical challenges remain in leveraging their lived experience for red-teaming while safeguarding psychological well-being. We conducted an empirical study of participatory red-teaming with 20 individuals stigmatized by stereotypes against nonprestigious college graduates in South Korea. Through mixed methods analysis, we found participants transformed experienced discrimination into strategic expertise for identifying biases, while facing psychological costs such as stress and negative reflections on group identity. Notably, red-team participation enhanced their sense of agency and empowerment through their role as guardians of the AI ecosystem. We discuss implications for designing participatory red-teaming that prioritizes both the ethical treatment and empowerment of stigmatized groups.

</details>


### [398] [The Neural-Wave Quick Escape Manual 2036: A Field Guide to Adversarial Living in the Era of "Empathic" AIoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19139)
*Boyuan Gu,Shuaiqi Cheng,Minghao yu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文通过设计虚构探讨2036年无摄像头毫米波传感平台如何以"关怀"为名监控老年人，提出通过对抗性策略手册来重新夺回隐私自主权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人口老龄化面临慢性护理赤字，家庭护理正被重塑为"光谱治理"。论文关注新兴的"共情AIoT"系统如何以关怀为名，通过毫米波传感技术监控老年人的非自愿微动作，从而剥夺个人自主权，将家庭转变为监控空间。

Method: 采用设计虚构方法，设定2036年的场景，引入名为Neural-Wave的无摄像头毫米波传感平台。通过一系列场景展示该系统如何运作，并创造了一个核心设计虚构产物：《Neural-Wave快速逃脱手册》。该手册作为面向老年人的非法指南，详细介绍了三种对抗策略：遵守、降级和拒绝，利用信号处理漏洞来重新获得家庭隐私。

Result: 论文展示了毫米波传感技术如何通过监测非自愿微动作来推断健康状况，将家庭转变为监控空间。通过设计虚构的手册，揭示了居民如何被迫"表演可读性"来重新获得基本自由。研究指出，在共情AIoT时代，隐私保护需要超越政策选择退出，而需要对抗性素养——即有意义地模糊自身数据痕迹的能力。

Conclusion: 在共情AIoT时代，隐私保护不仅需要政策层面的选择退出机制，更需要对抗性素养。当基础设施以"关怀"为名成为"监狱看守"时，个人需要具备通过对抗性策略来模糊自身数据痕迹的能力，以重新获得家庭隐私和自主权。论文通过设计虚构的手册形式，为老年人提供了对抗监控系统的具体战术工具。

Abstract: As the aging population faces a chronic care deficit, domestic care is increasingly recast as spectral governance. This paper presents a design fiction set in 2036, where the home is governed by Neural-Wave, a camera-free mmWave sensing platform that infers well-being from involuntary micro-motions. Through a set of scenarios, we illustrate how such empathic systems displace autonomy, forcing residents to perform legibility to regain basic freedoms. Our primary contribution is a diegetic artifact: The Neural-Wave Quick Escape Manual. Styled as an illicit guide for the elderly, it details adversarial tactics: structured around protocols to Comply, Degrade, and Refuse, that exploit signal processing vulnerabilities to reclaim domestic privacy. Through this artifact, we argue that in the era of empathic AIoT, privacy requires more than policy opt-outs; it demands adversarial literacy:the capacity to meaningfully obfuscate one's own data traces against an infrastructural jailer that calls itself care.

</details>


### [399] [As Content and Layout Co-Evolve: TangibleSite for Scaffolding Blind People's Webpage Design through Multimodal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19243)
*Jiasheng Li,Zining Zhang,Zeyu Yan,Matthew Wong,Arnav Mittal,Ge Gao,Huaishu Peng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TangibleSite：一款为盲人设计师开发的可访问网页设计工具，通过触觉、听觉和语音交互支持内容和布局的协同演化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲人在网页设计过程中面临挑战，需要同时生成内容和安排布局并进行迭代优化。现有工具难以支持盲人设计师在内容和布局协同演化过程中的需求，特别是缺乏将局部元素置于整体页面结构中的情境线索和多模态交互支持。

Method: 1. 与盲人参与者进行两轮协同设计会议，使用设计探针收集策略和支持需求；2. 基于研究发现开发TangibleSite工具，提供实时多模态反馈（触觉、听觉、语音交互）；3. 对6名盲人参与者进行形成性评估，测试工具效果。

Result: 研究发现盲人设计师偏好内容和布局协同演化，但需要外部支持来定位局部元素在整体结构中的位置。TangibleSite使盲人能够独立创建网页，支持内容和布局的协同优化，并减少了实现视觉一致性设计的障碍。

Conclusion: TangibleSite通过多模态交互支持盲人设计师在网页设计过程中实现内容和布局的协同演化，提高了可访问性和设计自主性，为无障碍设计工具开发提供了新思路。

Abstract: Creating webpages requires generating content and arranging layout while iteratively refining both to achieve a coherent design, a process that can be challenging for blind individuals. To understand how blind designers navigate this process, we conducted two rounds of co-design sessions with blind participants, using design probes to elicit their strategies and support needs. Our findings reveal a preference for content and layout to co-evolve, but this process requires external support through cues that situate local elements within the broader page structure as well as multimodal interactions. Building on these insights, we developed TangibleSite, an accessible web design tool that provides real-time multimodal feedback through tangible, auditory, and speech-based interactions. TangibleSite enables blind individuals to create, edit, and reposition webpage elements while integrating content and layout decisions. A formative evaluation with six blind participants demonstrated that TangibleSite enabled independent webpage creation, supported refinement across content and layout, and reduced barriers to achieving visually consistent designs.

</details>


### [400] [A Causal Framework for Estimating Heterogeneous Effects of On-Demand Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19296)
*Kirk Vanacore,Danielle R Thomas,Digory Smith,Bibi Groot,Justin Reich,Rene Kizilcec*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出可扩展因果推断框架，评估自适应学习系统中按需人工辅导的即时会话级效果，发现辅导能提高后续问题正确率约4个百分点，效果存在显著异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生在困难时寻求辅导导致传统评估受自选择和时变知识状态混杂，需要解决这些挑战来准确评估按需人工辅导的即时效果。

Method: 整合原则性分析样本构建与深度知识追踪(DKT)估计潜在掌握程度，然后使用因果森林进行双重稳健估计，应用于5000多个中学数学辅导会话。

Result: 请求人工辅导使下一个问题正确率提高约4个百分点，后续技能准确率提高约3个百分点，效果存在显著异质性（会话级效应范围-20.25pp至+19.91pp），对低先验掌握学生效果更大，低SES学生效果稍小。

Conclusion: 该框架为按需人工辅导的评估和持续改进提供了严谨实用的模板，可直接应用于新兴AI辅导系统，揭示了辅导效果的异质性和近迁移特性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a scalable causal inference framework for estimating the immediate, session-level effects of on-demand human tutoring embedded within adaptive learning systems. Because students seek assistance at moments of difficulty, conventional evaluation is confounded by self-selection and time-varying knowledge states. We address these challenges by integrating principled analytic sample construction with Deep Knowledge Tracing (DKT) to estimate latent mastery, followed by doubly robust estimation using Causal Forests. Applying this framework to over 5,000 middle-school mathematics tutoring sessions, we find that requesting human tutoring increases next-problem correctness by approximately 4 percentage points and accuracy on the subsequent skill encountered by approximately 3 percentage points, suggesting that the effects of tutoring have proximal transfer across knowledge components. This effect is robust to various forms of model specification and potential unmeasured confounders. Notably, these effects exhibit significant heterogeneity across sessions and students, with session-level effect estimates ranging from $-20.25pp$ to $+19.91pp$. Our follow-up analyses suggest that typical behavioral indicators, such as student talk time, do not consistently correlate with high-impact sessions. Furthermore, treatment effects are larger for students with lower prior mastery and slightly smaller for low-SES students. This framework offers a rigorous, practical template for the evaluation and continuous improvement of on-demand human tutoring, with direct applications for emerging AI tutoring systems.

</details>


### [401] [The Path to Conversational AI Tutors: Integrating Tutoring Best Practices and Targeted Technologies to Produce Scalable AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19303)
*Kirk Vanacore,Ryan S. Baker,Avery H. Closser,Jeremy Roschelle*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个"保留、改变、中心、研究"框架，用于指导对话式AI导师的设计，综合了人类辅导、传统ITS和生成式AI的优势，旨在开发可扩展、教学有效且关注学习社会动机维度的对话式辅导系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的出现加速了对话式辅导系统的发展，这些系统通过自然语言对话与学生互动。虽然传统智能辅导系统(ITS)主要作为自适应交互式问题集，但对话式导师有潜力通过实时参与学生的思考、问题和误解来模拟高质量的人类辅导。然而，实现对话式辅导的全部潜力需要综合考虑人类辅导研究、传统ITS的经验教训以及对话式AI导师的新兴工作。

Method: 采用"保留、改变、中心、研究"框架指导对话式辅导设计：保留传统ITS的已验证方法（如知识追踪和情感检测）；改变辅导交付方式，利用生成式AI进行动态内容生成和对话式支架；中心化意义建构、学生能动性和推理的细粒度诊断机会；并确定需要进一步研究的领域。

Result: 提出了一个综合框架，将人类辅导的成功原则、传统ITS的经验教训和新兴对话式AI技术相结合，为开发可扩展、教学有效且响应学习社会动机维度的对话式导师提供了研究议程。

Conclusion: 通过综合人类辅导、传统ITS和新兴生成式AI技术的见解，本文概述了开发对话式导师的研究议程，这些系统应具有可扩展性、教学有效性，并能响应学习的社会和动机维度，同时需要进一步研究效能测试、学生体验以及与人类教学的整合。

Abstract: The emergence of generative AI has accelerated the development of conversational tutoring systems that interact with students through natural language dialogue. Unlike prior intelligent tutoring systems (ITS), which largely function as adaptive and interactive problem sets with feedback and hints, conversational tutors hold the potential to simulate high-quality human tutoring by engaging with students' thoughts, questions, and misconceptions in real time. While some previous ITS, such as AutoTutor, could respond conversationally, they were expensive to author and lacked a full range of conversational ability. Generative AI has changed the capacity of ITS to engage conversationally. However, realizing the full potential of conversational tutors requires careful consideration of what research on human tutoring and ITS has already established, while also unpacking what new research will be needed. This paper synthesizes tenets of successful human tutoring, lessons learned from legacy ITS, and emerging work on conversational AI tutors. We use a keep, change, center, study framework for guiding the design of conversational tutoring. We argue that systems should keep proven methods from prior ITS, such as knowledge tracing and affect detection; change how tutoring is delivered by leveraging generative AI for dynamic content generation and dialogic scaffolding; and center opportunities for meaning-making, student agency, and granular diagnosis of reasoning. Finally, we identify areas requiring further study, including efficacy testing, student experience, and integration with human instruction. By synthesizing insights from human tutoring, legacy ITS, and emerging generative AI technologies, this paper outlines a research agenda for developing conversational tutors that are scalable, pedagogically effective, and responsive to the social and motivational dimensions of learning.

</details>


### [402] [Beyond Privacy Labels: How Users Perceive Different Information Sources for Understanding App's Privacy Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19352)
*Varun Shiri,Charles Liu,Keyu Yao,Jin L. C. Guo,Jinghui Cheng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索通过整合隐私政策、应用评论和社区评估等补充信息源来增强隐私标签，以解决现有隐私沟通方式的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管用户对隐私的关注日益增长，但现有隐私沟通方式存在严重缺陷：隐私政策冗长且充满法律术语，隐私标签则过于简化且不准确，导致用户难以理解产品隐私实践并评估其影响

Method: 探索将隐私政策、应用评论和社区评估等补充信息源与隐私标签整合的方法，通过用户研究评估不同信息源的有效性

Result: 用户研究发现，对信息源的感知有用性和信任度具有个体差异性，并受到过往经验的影响

Conclusion: 研究强调了考虑隐私实践的各种信息需求以及整合不同信息源以提供更有用隐私解决方案的重要性

Abstract: Despite having growing awareness and concerns about privacy, technology users are often insufficiently informed of the data practices of various digital products to protect themselves. Privacy policies and privacy labels, as two conventional ways of communicating data practices, are each criticized for important limitations -- one being lengthy and filled with legal jargon, and the other oversimplified and inaccurate -- causing users significant difficulty in understanding the privacy practices of the products and assessing their impact. To mitigate those issues, we explore ways to enhance privacy labels with the relevant content in complementary sources, including privacy policy, app reviews, and community-curated privacy assessments. Our user study results indicate that perceived usefulness and trust on those information sources are personal and influenced by past experience. Our work highlights the importance of considering various information needs for privacy practice and consolidating different sources for more useful privacy solutions.

</details>


### [403] [Policy or Community?: Supporting Individual Model Creators' Open Model Development in Model Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19354)
*Eun Jeong Kang,Fengyang Lin,Angel Hsing-Chi Hwang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 平台治理需考虑个体创作者实践：研究发现创作者工作流程中的三大监管需求，以及他们主要将负责任AI工具用于自我保护，责任感知受社区规范而非正式政策影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着轻量级微调技术和开放AI模型市场的发展，个体能够轻松构建和发布生成模型，但这也带来了有害和侵权内容的风险。平台现有的政策和负责任AI工具效果有限，因为创作者使用部分开放的模型，其开放性和透明度差异很大。研究旨在理解平台治理如何更好地支持负责任实践。

Method: 对19名个体模型创作者进行了半结构化访谈，分析他们的工作流程和实践。

Result: 识别出由创作者工作流程塑造的三大监管需求：减少下游危害、认可创作者贡献和原创性、确保模型所有权。创作者主要将负责任AI工具用于自我保护和可见性，他们的责任感深受社区规范而非正式政策影响。

Conclusion: 平台的治理决策必须考虑政策干预如何塑造个体创作者的实践和动机，社区规范在塑造责任感方面比正式政策更具影响力。

Abstract: Lightweight fine-tuning techniques and the rise of 'open' AI model marketplaces have enabled individuals to easily build and release generative models. Yet, this accessibility also raises risks, including the production of harmful and infringing content. While platforms offer policies and responsible AI tools, their effectiveness may be limited, as creators engage with partially open models that vary widely in openness and transparency. To understand how platform governance can better support responsible practices, we conducted semi-structured interviews with 19 individual model creators. We identified three regulatory needs shaped by creators' workflows: reducing downstream harms, recognizing creators' contributions and originality, and securing model ownership. Creators also repurpose RAI tools primarily for self-protection and visibility, and their sense of responsibility is deeply shaped by community norms rather than formal policies. We argue that platforms' governance decisions must consider how policy interventions shape the practices and motivations of individual creators.

</details>


### [404] [Reassurance Robots: OCD in the Age of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19401)
*Grace Barkhuff*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过分析100个Reddit帖子，探讨了生成式AI如何影响强迫症患者的症状表现，发现AI成为"安慰机器人"对患者有害，未来AI设计需要考虑强迫症因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI（如ChatGPT）的普及，强迫症患者出现了涉及AI的新颖强迫思维和行为。研究旨在探索AI如何影响强迫症的表现形式，以及当前AI设计对强迫症患者的负面影响。

Method: 采用探索性定性分析方法，从流行的强迫症相关Reddit子论坛中收集了100个涉及AI的帖子进行分析，研究AI如何影响强迫症的表现，包括新型的AI相关强迫思维和行为。

Result: 研究发现生成式AI在现有形式下成为"安慰机器人"，对强迫症患者造成伤害。分析揭示了涉及AI的新型强迫思维和行为的具体表现形式。

Conclusion: 当前形式的生成式AI对强迫症患者有害，未来的AI设计必须考虑强迫症因素，避免成为加剧患者症状的"安慰机器人"。

Abstract: Obsessive Compulsive Disorder (OCD) is a mental health disorder characterized by distressing repetitive patterns of thought, referred to as obsessions, and behaviors aimed to reduce the distress, referred to as compulsions. The explosion of artificial intelligence (AI) into the modern zeitgeist through the introduction of generative AI (GenAI) systems such as ChatGPT has led to novel obsessions and compulsions involving AI in individuals with OCD. Through an exploratory qualitative analysis of 100 Reddit posts related to AI on a popular subreddit for OCD, I examine the ways AI is impacting the presentation of OCD, including novel examples of AI-based obsessions and compulsions. I argue that GenAI in its current form harms individuals with OCD by becoming "Reassurance Robots," and that future designs of GenAI must take OCD into account.

</details>


### [405] [Positioning Modular Co-Design in Future HRI Design Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19422)
*Lingyun Chen,Qing Xiao,Zitao Zhang,Eli Blevis,Selma Šabanović*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出将模块化作为设计媒介，通过共设计活动探索长期人机关系，并建立PAS框架（个性化-适应性-可持续性）作为理解人们实践中如何实施模块化的人本视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前面向设计的HRI研究越来越关注机器人作为长期伴侣，但许多设计仍假设固定形态和稳定功能集。需要探索如何使长期人机关系变得可讨论和具体化，以支持更丰富的人机交互体验。

Method: 采用设计研究方法，通过一系列面向生命周期的共设计活动，让参与者使用模块化部件反复重新配置同一机器人以适应不同生命阶段，表达变化的需求、价值观和角色。

Result: 从共设计结果中提炼出PAS（个性化-适应性-可持续性）框架，作为理解人们实践中如何实施模块化的人本视角：为自我表达进行配置、跨过渡期适应、通过维修、重用和连续性维持机器人。

Conclusion: 提出了面向制造、社区可扩展的模块化平台的发展方向，并为面向设计的HRI工作提出了评估标准，优先考虑表达充分性、生命周期合理性、使用中可修复性和负责任的管理，而不仅仅是可用性或性能。

Abstract: Design-oriented HRI is increasingly interested in robots as long-term companions, yet many designs still assume a fixed form and a stable set of functions. We present an ongoing design research program that treats modularity as a designerly medium - a way to make long-term human-robot relationships discussable and material through co-design. Across a series of lifespan-oriented co-design activities, participants repeatedly reconfigured the same robot for different life stages, using modular parts to express changing needs, values, and roles. From these outcomes, we articulate PAS (Personalization-Adaptability-Sustainability) as a human-centered lens on how people enact modularity in practice: configuring for self-expression, adapting across transitions, and sustaining robots through repair, reuse, and continuity. We then sketch next steps toward a fabrication-aware, community-extensible modular platform and propose evaluation criteria for designerly HRI work that prioritize expressive adequacy, lifespan plausibility, repairability-in-use, and responsible stewardship - not only usability or performance.

</details>


### [406] [PuppetChat: Fostering Intimate Communication through Bidirectional Actions and Micronarratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19463)
*Emma Jiren Wang,Siying Hu,Zhicong Lu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: PuppetChat：通过具身交互恢复即时通讯表达深度的原型系统，使用互惠感知推荐器鼓励响应式互动，从用户故事生成个性化微叙事来增强亲密关系连接


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前即时通讯工具削弱了关怀表达，倾向于单次点击反应和模糊表情符号，这些工具不支持双向行动响应，无法保持交流持续不断的感觉，且与用户身份和共享内容关联较弱。需要恢复亲密关系中的表达深度。

Method: 开发PuppetChat双人消息原型系统，采用具身交互设计。系统包含互惠感知推荐器来鼓励响应式行动，并从用户故事生成个性化微叙事，将互动根植于个人历史中。

Result: 对11对亲密伴侣或朋友的10天实地研究显示，该方法增强了社交临场感，支持更具表达性的自我披露，并维持了连续性和共享记忆。

Conclusion: PuppetChat通过具身交互、互惠感知推荐和个性化微叙事，成功恢复了即时通讯中的表达深度，为亲密关系的数字连接提供了更丰富的交互体验。

Abstract: As a primary channel for sustaining modern intimate relationships, instant messaging facilitates frequent connection across distances. However, today's tools often dilute care; they favor single tap reactions and vague emojis that do not support two way action responses, do not preserve the feeling that the exchange keeps going without breaking, and are weakly tied to who we are and what we share. To address this challenge, we present PuppetChat, a dyadic messaging prototype that restores this expressive depth through embodied interaction. PuppetChat uses a reciprocity aware recommender to encourage responsive actions and generates personalized micronarratives from user stories to ground interactions in personal history. Our 10-day field study with 11 dyads of close partners or friends revealed that this approach enhanced social presence, supported more expressive self disclosure, and sustained continuity and shared memories.

</details>


### [407] [Sound-first immersive training for blind and low-vision learners: A simulation flow for safe, standardized orientation, mobility, and daily living practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19554)
*Daniel A. Muñoz*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出基于空间音频和声化的沉浸式训练流程，用于视障人士的定向行走和日常生活技能培训，通过参数化场景模板、紧凑的提示词汇和轻量安全协议实现标准化可重复训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视障人士定向行走培训依赖教师资源、物理模型和变化的外部环境，难以标准化和规模化重复。需要一种能够减少教师负担、实现安全重复训练并支持跨康复中心统一标准的解决方案。

Method: 采用声音优先的沉浸式训练流程：1) 参数化场景模板（如信号灯路口、公共交通乘车、厨房任务）；2) 紧凑一致的提示词汇，具有清晰的频谱位置和时序以避免掩蔽；3) 轻量安全协议，支持分级暴露、内容警告、坐姿开始、退出选项和结构化汇报；4) 基于头戴设备的高质量双耳渲染和头部追踪；5) 使用3D场景几何作为隐形支架来锚定声源、触发事件、定义风险/引导区域并控制物理合理的运动。

Result: 虽然未报告具体行为结果，但提出了一个可实施的训练流程，整合了听觉科学与康复中心就绪的设计，为标准化评估和未来比较研究提供了实用基础。系统通过提示密度、事件节奏和任务复杂度调节训练难度，同时保持提示一致性以促进跨场景的技能迁移。

Conclusion: 该技术方案旨在实现安全重复训练、减轻教师负担、支持跨康复中心的统一标准，并解决HRTF个性化、评估标准和可访问性集成方面的现有差距。声音优先的交互对于视障用户至关重要，该流程为标准化评估和未来研究提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Orientation and mobility (O&M) instruction for blind and low-vision learners is effective but difficult to standardize and repeat at scale due to the reliance on instructor availability, physical mock-ups, and variable real-world outdoor conditions. This Technical Note presents a sound-first immersive training flow that uses spatial audio and sonification as the primary channel for action and feedback in pre-street O&M and daily-living practice. The approach specifies parameterized scenario templates (e.g., signalized street crossing, public transport boarding, and kitchen tasks), a compact and consistent cue vocabulary with clear spectral placement and timing to mitigate masking, and a lightweight safety protocol enabling graded exposure, content warnings, seated starts, opt-outs, and structured debriefs. The system assumes a head-mounted device with high-quality binaural rendering and head tracking; 3D scene geometry is used as an invisible scaffold to anchor sources, trigger events, define risk/guidance volumes, and govern physically plausible motion without visuals. Session difficulty is shaped via cue density, event tempo, and task complexity while preserving cue consistency to promote transfer across scenarios. The specification aims to enable safe repetition, reduce instructor burden, and support clearer standards across rehabilitation centers, aligning with evidence that audio-first interaction is essential for blind and visually impaired users and addressing gaps in HRTF personalization, evaluation standards, and accessibility integration. Although no behavioral outcomes are reported here, this implementable flow consolidates auditory science with center-ready design, offering a pragmatic foundation for standardized evaluation and future comparative studies.

</details>


### [408] [Identifying, Explaining, and Correcting Ableist Language with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19560)
*Kynnedy Simone Smith,Lydia B. Chilton,Danielle Bragg*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨LLM（特别是ChatGPT）在识别和纠正残障歧视语言方面的潜力，通过与残障社群成员标注对比，发现AI在叙事一致性和可访问性方面更受偏好，但人类标注在情感深度和文化基础方面仍有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残障歧视语言持续传播有害刻板印象和排斥，但其微妙性使其难以识别和解决。人工智能可作为对抗残障歧视语言的有力工具，提供检测和替代偏见术语的能力。

Method: 采用两部分研究设计：首先比较GPT-4o生成内容与经过培训的残障社群成员的众包标注；然后邀请残障参与者评估两者。研究对比了AI和人类在识别和纠正残障歧视语言方面的表现。

Result: 参与者对AI和人类标注的同意程度相同，但显著偏好AI标注，认为其叙事一致性和可访问性风格更好。同时，他们重视人类标注的情感深度和文化基础。研究创建了微妙的残障歧视标注数据集。

Conclusion: LLM在处理文化敏感内容方面既有前景也有局限。AI在识别和纠正残障歧视语言方面表现出色，但人类的情感深度和文化理解仍不可替代。研究为包容性写作工具提供了设计考虑。

Abstract: Ableist language perpetuates harmful stereotypes and exclusion, yet its nuanced nature makes it difficult to recognize and address. Artificial intelligence could serve as a powerful ally in the fight against ableist language, offering tools that detect and suggest alternatives to biased terms. This two-part study investigates the potential of large language models (LLMs), specifically ChatGPT, to rectify ableist language and educate users about inclusive communication. We compared GPT-4o generations with crowdsourced annotations from trained disability community members, then invited disabled participants to evaluate both. Participants reported equal agreement with human and AI annotations but significantly preferred the AI, citing its narrative consistency and accessible style. At the same time, they valued the emotional depth and cultural grounding of human annotations. These findings highlight the promise and limits of LLMs in handling culturally sensitive content. Our contributions include a dataset of nuanced ableism annotations and design considerations for inclusive writing tools.

</details>


### [409] [Cooperation After the Algorithm: Designing Human-AI Coexistence Beyond the Illusion of Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19629)
*Tatia Codreanu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出人类-AI合作生态框架，通过治理基础设施解决AI不承担责任的结构性不对称问题，确保可持续的负责任合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统越来越多地参与高价值领域，但其不承担责任、不承担风险的结构性不对称已导致制裁、专业错误和治理失败。需要建立稳定的治理基础设施来分配剩余风险。

Method: 结合制度分析和进化合作理论，引入形式不等式确定AI依赖产生正期望合作价值的条件，提出合作生态框架的六个设计原则，并通过三个政策工具（人类-AI合作宪章、违约风险登记册、合作准备度审计）实现操作化。

Result: 建立了形式化模型，明确治理条件、系统政策和问责制度如何共同决定合作是否理性或存在结构性缺陷，将分析单位从用户-AI二元关系扩展到塑造激励、信号、问责和修复的制度环境。

Conclusion: 论文为设计能够维持负责任、可信赖合作的人类-AI系统提供了理论基础和实践工具包，强调稳定的人类-AI共存是制度成就，依赖于能够分配剩余风险的治理基础设施。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence systems increasingly participate in research, law, education, media, and governance. Their fluent and adaptive outputs create an experience of collaboration. However, these systems do not bear responsibility, incur liability, or share stakes in downstream consequences. This structural asymmetry has already produced sanctions, professional errors, and governance failures in high-stakes contexts We argue that stable human-AI coexistence is an institutional achievement that depends on governance infrastructure capable of distributing residual risk. Drawing on institutional analysis and evolutionary cooperation theory, we introduce a formal inequality that specifies when reliance on AI yields positive expected cooperative value. The model makes explicit how governance conditions, system policy, and accountability regimes jointly determine whether cooperation is rational or structurally defective. From this formalization we derive a cooperation ecology framework with six design principles: reciprocity contracts, visible trust infrastructure, conditional cooperation modes, defection-mitigation mechanisms, narrative literacy against authority theatre, and an Earth-first sustainability constraint. We operationalize the framework through three policy artefacts: a Human-AI Cooperation Charter, a Defection Risk Register, and a Cooperation Readiness Audit. Together, these elements shift the unit of analysis from the user-AI dyad to the institutional environment that shapes incentives, signals, accountability, and repair. The paper provides a theoretical foundation and practical toolkit for designing human-AI systems that can sustain accountable, trustworthy cooperation over time.

</details>


### [410] ["The explanation makes sense": An Empirical Study on LLM Performance in News Classification and its Influence on Judgment in Human-AI Collaborative Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19690)
*Qile Wang,Prerana Khatiwada,Avinash Chouhan,Ashrey Mahesh,Joy Mwaria,Duy Duc Tran,Kenneth E. Barner,Matthew Louis Mauriello*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索使用大语言模型（LLM）对美国新闻进行政治意识形态分类，并评估AI解释对人类决策的影响，发现AI辅助显著提高用户信心，详细解释更具说服力且更可能改变决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 媒体偏见传播是重要问题，政治话语影响信念和观点。虽然大语言模型可以扩展分类任务，但其可信度仍存疑虑。为推进人机协作，研究探索使用LLM进行新闻政治意识形态分类的可行性及其对用户决策的影响。

Method: 1. 比较GPT模型（通过提示工程）与最先进的监督机器学习在34k公开数据集上的表现；2. 收集17k新闻文章，测试GPT-4预测并提供简要和详细解释；3. 进行组间研究（N=124），评估LLM生成的解释如何影响人类标注、判断和信心。

Result: AI辅助显著提高用户信心（p<.001），详细解释比简要解释更具说服力，更可能改变人类决策。通过主题分析提出了AI解释的建议，并提供了数据集供进一步研究。

Conclusion: 研究证明了使用LLM进行新闻政治意识形态分类的可行性，并揭示了AI解释对人类决策的重要影响。详细解释能更有效地影响用户判断，为设计有效的人机协作系统提供了实证依据。

Abstract: The spread of media bias is a significant concern as political discourse shapes beliefs and opinions. Addressing this challenge computationally requires improved methods for interpreting news. While large language models (LLMs) can scale classification tasks, concerns remain about their trustworthiness. To advance human-AI collaboration, we investigate the feasibility of using LLMs to classify U.S. news by political ideology and examine their effect on user decision-making. We first compared GPT models with prompt engineering to state-of-the-art supervised machine learning on a 34k public dataset. We then collected 17k news articles and tested GPT-4 predictions with brief and detailed explanations. In a between-subjects study (N=124), we evaluated how LLM-generated explanations influence human annotation, judgment, and confidence. Results show that AI assistance significantly increases confidence ($p<.001$), with detailed explanations more persuasive and more likely to alter decisions. We highlight recommendations for AI explanations through thematic analysis and provide our dataset for further research.

</details>


### [411] [Git Takes Two: Split-View Awareness for Collaborative Learning of Distributed Workflows in Git](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19714)
*Joel Bucher,Lahari Goswami,Sverrir Thorgeirsson,April Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GitAcademy：一个基于浏览器的Git学习平台，采用分屏协作模式，让学习者能在本地仓库工作同时实时看到伙伴的操作，作为Git协作训练的模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Git虽然广泛用于协作软件开发，但对新手来说很有挑战性。现有学习工具大多关注个人工作流，而Git本质上是协作的，需要专门的协作训练工具。

Method: 开发GitAcademy平台，嵌入完整的Git环境，采用分屏协作模式：学习者操作自己的本地仓库连接到共享远程仓库，同时实时镜像显示伙伴的操作。通过13对学习者的组内研究，比较分屏界面与单屏基线的效果。

Result: 分屏界面增强了社交临场感，支持同伴教学，学习者一致偏好分屏界面而非单屏基线，尽管性能提升效果不一。分屏意识可作为Git和其他分布式技术系统协作学习的训练支架。

Conclusion: 分屏协作模式作为专门的训练模拟器，能有效培养分布式状态意识、协调能力和协作问题解决能力，为Git等分布式技术系统的协作学习提供了有效的训练支架。

Abstract: Git is widely used for collaborative software development, but it can be challenging for newcomers. While most learning tools focus on individual workflows, Git is inherently collaborative. We present GitAcademy, a browser-based learning platform that embeds a full Git environment with a split-view collaborative mode: learners work on their own local repositories connected to a shared remote repository, while simultaneously seeing their partner's actions mirrored in real time. This design is not intended for everyday software development, but rather as a training simulator to build awareness of distributed states, coordination, and collaborative troubleshooting. In a within-subjects study with 13 pairs of learners, we found that the split-view interface enhanced social presence, supported peer teaching, and was consistently preferred over a single-view baseline, even though performance gains were mixed. We further discuss how split-view awareness can serve as a training-only scaffold for collaborative learning of Git and other distributed technical systems.

</details>


### [412] [Unfolding Ordered Matrices into BioFabric Motifs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19745)
*Jules Wulms,Wouter Meulemans,Bettina Speckmann*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有序矩阵的BioFabric布局算法，通过Moran's I排序邻接矩阵并检测模式，然后"展开"为高质量的BioFabric可视化，可处理最多250个顶点的大图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BioFabric是一种清晰绘制大图的方法，其视觉质量依赖于顶点和边的排序。目前缺乏能自动识别模式并提供合适排序的高效布局算法，以将模式表达为motif。

Method: 使用Moran's I对输入图的邻接矩阵进行排序，利用作者最近的算法检测噪声模式，然后将有序矩阵及其模式"展开"为高质量的BioFabric可视化。

Result: 提出的流程能够高效地为BioFabric找到良好的顶点和边排序，可以处理最多250个顶点的大图，生成高质量的BioFabric可视化。

Conclusion: 通过有序矩阵方法，实现了自动化的BioFabric布局算法，能够有效识别和表达图中的模式，解决了之前缺乏高效自动布局算法的问题。

Abstract: BioFabrics were introduced by Longabaugh in 2012 as a way to draw large graphs in a clear and uncluttered manner. The visual quality of BioFabrics crucially depends on the order of vertices and edges, which can be chosen independently. Effective orders can expose salient patterns, which in turn can be summarized by motifs, allowing users to take in complex networks at-a-glance. However, so far there is no efficient layout algorithm which automatically recognizes patterns and delivers both a vertex and an edge ordering that allows these patterns to be expressed as motifs. In this paper we show how to use well-ordered matrices as a tool to efficiently find good vertex and edge orders for BioFabrics. Specifically, we order the adjacency matrix of the input graph using Moran's $I$ and detect (noisy) patterns with our recent algorithm. In this note we show how to "unfold" the ordered matrix and its patterns into a high-quality BioFabric. Our pipelines easily handles graphs with up to 250 vertices.

</details>


### [413] [Ambient Analytics: Calm Technology for Immersive Visualization and Sensemaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19809)
*Sebastian Hubenschmid,Arvind Srinivasan,Niklas Elmqvist,Dieter Schmalstieg,Michael Sedlmair*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索如何通过增强现实将可视化和谐地融入日常生活，从视觉分析转向环境分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强现实在嵌入数据可视化方面具有巨大潜力，但存在信息过载风险；平静技术旨在将信息置于用户注意力外围，减少认知负荷

Method: 通过增强现实技术，将可视化从需要专注参与的视觉分析转变为融入日常生活的环境分析

Result: 探索了增强现实中可视化设计的新范式，从主动交互转向被动感知，实现信息与环境的和谐融合

Conclusion: 增强现实可视化应借鉴平静技术理念，减少认知负荷，实现从视觉分析到环境分析的转变，创造更自然的用户体验

Abstract: Augmented reality has great potential for embedding data visualizations in the world around the user. While this can enhance users' understanding of their surroundings, it also bears the risk of overwhelming their senses with a barrage of information. In contrast, calm technologies aim to place information in the user's attentional periphery, minimizing cognitive load instead of demanding focused engagement. In this column, we explore how visualizations can be harmoniously integrated into our everyday life through augmented reality, progressing from visual analytics to ambient analytics.

</details>


### [414] [Progressive Value Reading: The Use of Motion to Gradually Examine Data Involving Large Magnitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19853)
*Leni Yang,Aymeric Ferron,Yvonne Jansen,Pierre Dragicevic*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出"渐进式数值读取"概念，通过运动（如长距离滚动、长时间交互）让用户体验极值数据，将数值差异转化为持续时间和努力感受


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法（如对数尺度、多尺度可视化）在处理极大/极小值或多数量级范围数据时存在局限性，需要探索新的设计策略来帮助用户更直观地理解极端数值

Method: 收集55个现实和假设的可视化案例，分析其中使用运动来渐进式读取数值的设计模式，提出"渐进式数值读取"概念，并构建包含10个设计维度的设计空间

Result: 建立了渐进式数值读取的设计框架，提供了共享词汇表、新颖技术灵感，并为实证评估奠定了基础，同时创建了可供探索的在线案例库

Conclusion: 通过运动来体验数值的设计策略虽然尚未充分探索，但为数据可视化提供了新的机会，特别是在帮助用户理解极端数值方面具有潜力

Abstract: People often struggle to interpret data with extremely large or small values, or ranges spanning multiple orders of magnitude. While traditional approaches, such as log scales and multiscale visualizations, can help, we explore in this article a different approach used in some emerging designs: the use of motion to let viewers gradually experience magnitude -- for example, interactive graphics that require long scrolling or street paintings stretching hundreds of meters. This approach typically demands substantial time and sustained interaction, translating differences in magnitude into a visceral sense of duration and effort. Although largely underexplored, this design strategy offers new opportunities. We introduce the term progressive value reading to refer to the use of motion to progressively examine an information object that encodes a value, where the amount of motion reflects the value. We compiled a corpus of 55 real-life and hypothetical visualization examples that allow, encourage, or require progressive value reading. From this corpus, we derived a design space of ten design dimensions, providing a shared vocabulary, inspiration for novel techniques, and a foundation for empirical evaluation. An online corpus is also available for exploration.

</details>


### [415] [Protecting and Promoting Human Agency in Education in the Age of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20014)
*Olga Viberg,Mutlu Cukurova,Rene F. Kizilcec,Simon Buckingham Shum,Dorottya Demszky,Dragan Gašević,Thorben Jansen,Ioana Jivet,Jelena Jovanovic,Jennifer Meyer,Kou Murayama,Zach Pardos,Chris Piech,Nikol Rummel,Naomi E. Winstone*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该会议报告综合跨学科见解，概念化了界定人类能动性的四个方面，探讨了在教育中保护和发展人类能动性的实践困境，重点关注规范性约束、透明度和认知卸载，强调了关键张力及对教育中AI伦理有效整合的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能动性在教育中至关重要，但生成式AI的使用日益挑战这一核心价值。研究旨在探索如何在AI时代保护和发展人类能动性，为教育中AI的伦理有效整合提供指导。

Method: 采用会议报告形式，综合跨学科见解，概念化人类能动性的四个关键方面：人类监督、AI-人类互补性、AI能力、关系涌现。通过探索实践困境，聚焦规范性约束、透明度和认知卸载等维度。

Result: 识别了保护和发展人类能动性的关键实践困境，揭示了规范性约束、透明度和认知卸载等方面的核心张力，为教育中AI整合提供了重要的伦理和实践启示。

Conclusion: 人类能动性在AI时代教育中面临严峻挑战，需要通过多维度框架理解和保护。报告提出的四个方面和实践困境分析为教育中AI的伦理有效整合提供了重要指导，强调需要在技术整合中平衡人类主体性和技术辅助作用。

Abstract: Human agency is crucial in education and increasingly challenged by the use of generative AI. This meeting report synthesizes interdisciplinary insights and conceptualizes four aspects that delineate human agency: human oversight, AI-human complementarity, AI competencies, and relational emergence. We explore practical dilemmas for protecting and promoting agency, focusing on normative constraints, transparency, and cognitive offloading, and highlight key tensions and implications to inform ethical and effective AI integration in education.

</details>


### [416] [Studying the Separability of Visual Channel Pairs in Symbol Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20022)
*Poorna Talkad Sukumar,Maurizio Porfiri,Oded Nov*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过众包实验评估了双变量符号地图中四种视觉通道组合的可分离性，发现颜色×形状组合最易分离，尺寸×方向组合最难分离，且可分离性存在不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可视化通常通过将属性映射到不同的视觉通道（如颜色、尺寸、形状）来编码多变量数据。这些编码的有效性取决于可分离性——即通道能够被独立感知的程度。然而，特别是在基于地图的背景下，缺乏关于可分离性的系统性证据。

Method: 采用众包实验方法，在双变量符号地图的背景下评估了四种通道组合的可分离性：颜色（有序）×形状、颜色（有序）×尺寸、尺寸×形状、尺寸×方向。通过准确性和速度分析来测量可分离性。

Result: 准确性和速度分析均显示：颜色×形状是最可分离的组合，尺寸×方向是最不可分离的组合，而尺寸×颜色和尺寸×形状之间没有显著差异。可分离性具有不对称性——性能取决于哪个通道编码任务相关变量，颜色和形状的表现优于尺寸，正方形形状尤其难以区分。

Conclusion: 研究结果推进了对视觉可分离性的实证理解，对多变量地图设计具有重要意义，为设计更有效的可视化提供了基于证据的指导。

Abstract: Visualizations often encode multivariate data by mapping attributes to distinct visual channels such as color, size, or shape. The effectiveness of these encodings depends on separability--the extent to which channels can be perceived independently. Yet systematic evidence for separability, especially in map-based contexts, is lacking. We present a crowdsourced experiment that evaluates the separability of four channel pairs--color (ordered) x shape, color (ordered) x size, size x shape, and size x orientation--in the context of bivariate symbol maps. Both accuracy and speed analyses show that color x shape is the most separable and size x orientation the least separable, while size x color and size x shape do not differ. Separability also proved asymmetric--performance depended on which channel encoded the task-relevant variable, with color and shape outperforming size, and square shape especially difficult to discriminate. Our findings advance the empirical understanding of visual separability, with implications for multivariate map design.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [417] [Health+: Empowering Individuals via Unifying Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19319)
*Sujaya Maiyya,Shantanu Sharma,Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: Health+是一个以用户为中心的多模态健康数据管理系统，旨在解决个人医疗数据碎片化问题，让个人能够跨模态上传、查询和共享健康数据，强调个体控制而非机构改革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗生态系统是碎片化且以机构为中心的，个人缺乏对分散在不同不兼容系统和格式中的医疗记录的有意义控制。这阻碍了个人有效管理自己的健康数据。

Method: 提出Health+系统，采用用户中心设计，提供直观界面和智能推荐，支持跨模态（文本、图像、报告等）数据上传、查询和共享。在系统层面处理异构健康记录的存储、集成和安全问题，确保效率和隐私。

Result: 通过统一多模态数据并优先考虑患者需求，Health+为建立更互联、可解释和用户控制的健康信息生态系统奠定了基础。系统强调个体自主权，使包括技术能力有限的用户也能有效管理健康数据。

Conclusion: Health+通过以用户为中心的方法解决了个人健康数据管理的挑战，为创建更连接、可解释和用户控制的健康信息生态系统提供了框架，强调个体自主权而非机构改革。

Abstract: Managing personal health data is a challenge in today's fragmented and institution-centric healthcare ecosystem. Individuals often lack meaningful control over their medical records, which are scattered across incompatible systems and formats. This vision paper presents Health+, a user-centric, multimodal health data management system that empowers individuals (including those with limited technical expertise) to upload, query, and share their data across modalities (e.g., text, images, reports). Rather than aiming for institutional overhaul, Health+ emphasizes individual agency by providing intuitive interfaces and intelligent recommendations for data access and sharing. At the system level, it tackles the complexity of storing, integrating, and securing heterogeneous health records, ensuring both efficiency and privacy. By unifying multimodal data and prioritizing patients, Health+ lays the foundation for a more connected, interpretable, and user-controlled health information ecosystem.

</details>


### [418] [Tri-Subspaces Disentanglement for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19585)
*Chunlei Meng,Jiabin Luo,Zhenglin Yan,Zhenyu Yu,Rong Fu,Zhongxue Gan,Chun Ouyang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: TSD框架通过三子空间解耦（公共、子模态共享、私有）和子空间感知交叉注意力融合，提升多模态情感分析的表示能力，在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSA方法主要关注全局共享表示或模态特定特征，忽略了仅由某些模态对共享的信号，这限制了多模态表示的表达能力和判别力。

Method: 提出三子空间解耦框架：1) 公共子空间捕捉全局一致性；2) 子模态共享子空间建模成对跨模态协同；3) 私有子空间保留模态特定线索。使用解耦监督器和结构化正则化损失保持子空间纯净独立，并设计子空间感知交叉注意力融合模块自适应整合三个子空间信息。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI上达到0.691 MAE，在CMU-MOSEI上达到54.9% ACC-7，均实现SOTA性能。在多模态意图识别任务上也表现出良好的迁移能力。消融研究证实三子空间解耦和SACA共同增强了对多粒度跨模态情感线索的建模。

Conclusion: TSD框架通过显式分解特征到三个互补子空间并自适应融合，有效解决了现有MSA方法在表示能力上的局限性，在多模态情感分析和意图识别任务上均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) integrates language, visual, and acoustic modalities to infer human sentiment. Most existing methods either focus on globally shared representations or modality-specific features, while overlooking signals that are shared only by certain modality pairs. This limits the expressiveness and discriminative power of multimodal representations. To address this limitation, we propose a Tri-Subspace Disentanglement (TSD) framework that explicitly factorizes features into three complementary subspaces: a common subspace capturing global consistency, submodally-shared subspaces modeling pairwise cross-modal synergies, and private subspaces preserving modality-specific cues. To keep these subspaces pure and independent, we introduce a decoupling supervisor together with structured regularization losses. We further design a Subspace-Aware Cross-Attention (SACA) fusion module that adaptively models and integrates information from the three subspaces to obtain richer and more robust representations. Experiments on CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI demonstrate that TSD achieves state-of-the-art performance across all key metrics, reaching 0.691 MAE on CMU-MOSI and 54.9% ACC-7 on CMU-MOSEI, and also transfers well to multimodal intent recognition tasks. Ablation studies confirm that tri-subspace disentanglement and SACA jointly enhance the modeling of multi-granular cross-modal sentiment cues.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [419] [FineRef: Fine-Grained Error Reflection and Correction for Long-Form Generation with Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18437)
*Yixing Peng,Licheng Zhang,Shancheng Fang,Yi Liu,Peijian Gu,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FineRef是一个基于细粒度错误反思的框架，通过两阶段训练策略教导模型自我识别和纠正引用错误（不匹配和无关），显著提升引用性能和答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs在生成引用时经常产生不匹配或不相关的引用，现有方法过度优化引用保真度而忽视与用户查询的相关性，导致答案质量下降。此外，单次生成范式在需要多个引用的长文本生成中表现不佳。

Method: FineRef采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段通过监督微调建立"尝试-反思-纠正"行为模式，使用轻量级模型构建的细粒度可控反思数据，并通过在线自我反思引导策略迭代丰富训练数据；第二阶段应用过程级强化学习，采用多维奖励方案提升反思准确性、答案质量和纠正效果。

Result: 在ALCE基准测试中，FineRef显著提升引用性能和答案准确性。7B模型在Citation F1上比GPT-4提升18%，在EM Recall上提升4%，并在关键评估指标上超越最先进模型。在领域迁移和噪声检索场景中也表现出强大的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FineRef通过细粒度错误反思有效解决了LLMs引用生成中的不匹配和无关问题，通过两阶段训练策略显著提升了引用准确性和答案质量，在多个场景下表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Generating with citations is crucial for trustworthy Large Language Models (LLMs), yet even advanced LLMs often produce mismatched or irrelevant citations. Existing methods over-optimize citation fidelity while overlooking relevance to the user query, which degrades answer quality and robustness in real-world settings with noisy or irrelevant retrieved content. Moreover, the prevailing single-pass paradigm struggles to deliver optimal answers in long-form generation that requiring multiple citations. To address these limitations, we propose FineRef, a framework based on Fine-grained error Reflection, which explicitly teaches the model to self-identify and correct two key citation errors, mismatch and irrelevance, on a per-citation basis. FineRef follows a two-stage training strategy. The first stage instills an "attempt-reflect-correct" behavioral pattern via supervised fine-tuning, using fine-grained and controllable reflection data constructed by specialized lightweight models. An online self-reflective bootstrapping strategy is designed to improve generalization by iteratively enriching training data with verified, self-improving examples. To further enhance the self-reflection and correction capability, the second stage applies process-level reinforcement learning with a multi-dimensional reward scheme that promotes reflection accuracy, answer quality, and correction gain. Experiments on the ALCE benchmark demonstrate that FineRef significantly improves both citation performance and answer accuracy. Our 7B model outperforms GPT-4 by up to 18% in Citation F1 and 4% in EM Recall, while also surpassing the state-of-the-art model across key evaluation metrics. FineRef also exhibits strong generalization and robustness in domain transfer settings and noisy retrieval scenarios.

</details>


### [420] [Altar: Structuring Sharable Experimental Data from Early Exploration to Publication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18588)
*William Gaultier,Andrea Lodetti,Ian Coghill,David Colliaux,Maximilian Fleck,Alienor Lahlou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Altar是一个轻量级、领域无关的框架，用于在实验项目开发阶段结构化实验数据，基于Sacred实验跟踪模型，结合NoSQL数据库和专用存储，支持不同技能用户，连接探索性实验与FAIR数据共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验项目开发阶段的数据和元数据管理在协作研究中具有挑战性，常被项目提案中的数据管理计划忽视，但这对确保可重复性和避免发表时追溯重建至关重要。

Method: 基于Sacred实验跟踪模型构建轻量级框架，将参数、元数据、曲线和小文件存储在灵活的NoSQL数据库中，大型原始数据保存在专用存储并通过唯一标识符链接，与现有工作流可组合集成。

Result: Altar框架支持不同技能用户（博士生、博士后、PI、实验室管理员、系统管理员）的使用路径，无需专门基础设施即可开始使用，可轻松部署到服务器并公开访问，支持项目扩展和数据发布准备。

Conclusion: Altar通过解决研究的动态阶段，为探索性实验与FAIR对齐的数据共享提供了实用桥梁，填补了数据管理计划中的空白。

Abstract: Managing the data and metadata during the active development phase of an experimental project presents a significant challenge, particularly in collaborative research. This phase is frequently overlooked in Data Management Plans included in project proposals, despite its important role in ensuring reproducibility and preventing the need for retroactive reconstruction at the time of publication. Here we present Altar, a lightweight, domain-agnostic framework for structuring experimental data from the onset of a project without imposing rigid data models. Altar is built around the Sacred experiment-tracking model and captures experimental (meta)data and structures them. Parameters, metadata, curves and small files are stored in a flexible NoSQL database, while large raw data are maintained in dedicated storage and linked through unique identifiers, ensuring efficiency and traceability. This integration is composable with exiting workflows, allowing integration with minimial disruption of work habits. We document different pathways to use Altar based on users skillset (PhD students, Post-docs, Principal Investigators, Laboratory administrators, System administrators). While getting started with Altar does not require a specialized infrastructure, the framework can be easily deployed on a server and made publicly accessible when scaling up or preparing data for publication. By addressing the dynamic phase of research, Altar provides a practical bridge between exploratory experimentation and FAIR-aligned data sharing.

</details>


### [421] [Towards Reliable Negative Sampling for Recommendation with Implicit Feedback via In-Community Popularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18759)
*Chen Chen,Haobo Lin,Yuanbo Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ICPNS是一种基于社区流行度的负采样框架，利用用户社区结构和社区内流行度来识别可靠的负样本，提升隐式反馈推荐系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐式反馈推荐系统中，只有正交互被观察到，缺乏显式负信号。负采样对模型训练至关重要，但设计同时确保真实性、难度和可解释性的负采样策略具有挑战性。

Method: 提出ICPNS框架，利用用户社区结构识别可靠负样本。基于物品曝光由潜在用户社区驱动的洞察，通过识别这些社区并利用社区内流行度来近似物品曝光概率。在用户社区内流行但未被点击的物品被视为更可靠的真正负样本。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，ICPNS在图基推荐器上带来一致改进，在基于矩阵分解的模型上具有竞争力，在统一评估协议下优于代表性负采样策略。

Conclusion: ICPNS通过利用用户社区结构和社区内流行度，有效解决了隐式反馈推荐系统中的负采样挑战，提供了一种可靠且信息丰富的负样本识别方法。

Abstract: Learning from implicit feedback is a fundamental problem in modern recommender systems, where only positive interactions are observed and explicit negative signals are unavailable. In such settings, negative sampling plays a critical role in model training by constructing negative items that enable effective preference learning and ranking optimization. However, designing reliable negative sampling strategies remains challenging, as they must simultaneously ensure realness, hardness, and interpretability. To this end, we propose \textbf{ICPNS (In-Community Popularity Negative Sampling)}, a novel framework that leverages user community structure to identify reliable and informative negative samples. Our approach is grounded in the insight that item exposure is driven by latent user communities. By identifying these communities and utilizing in-community popularity, ICPNS effectively approximates the probability of item exposure. Consequently, items that are popular within a user's community but remain unclicked are identified as more reliable true negatives. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that ICPNS yields consistent improvements on graph-based recommenders and competitive performance on MF-based models, outperforming representative negative sampling strategies under a unified evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [422] [Adaptive Multi-Agent Reasoning for Text-to-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19040)
*Jiaxin Wu,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出自适应多智能体检索框架，通过动态协调专门智能体进行多轮推理，解决文本到视频检索中的查询依赖时序推理问题，在TRECVid基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频平台和多模态大语言模型的兴起增加了对可扩展、有效的零样本文本到视频检索系统的需求。现有方法在处理涉及时序、逻辑或因果关系的复杂查询时，仍面临查询依赖时序推理的挑战。

Method: 提出自适应多智能体检索框架，包括：检索智能体（大规模视频库检索）、推理智能体（零样本上下文时序推理）、查询重构智能体（精炼模糊查询）。这些智能体由编排智能体动态协调，利用中间反馈和推理结果指导执行。引入包含检索性能记忆和历史推理轨迹的新型通信机制以改进协调和决策。

Result: 在跨越八年的三个TRECVid基准测试中，该框架相比CLIP4Clip实现了两倍提升，并大幅超越最先进方法。

Conclusion: 自适应多智能体框架通过动态协调专门智能体进行多轮推理，有效解决了文本到视频检索中的查询依赖时序推理问题，显著提升了复杂查询的检索性能。

Abstract: The rise of short-form video platforms and the emergence of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have amplified the need for scalable, effective, zero-shot text-to-video retrieval systems. While recent advances in large-scale pretraining have improved zero-shot cross-modal alignment, existing methods still struggle with query-dependent temporal reasoning, limiting their effectiveness on complex queries involving temporal, logical, or causal relationships. To address these limitations, we propose an adaptive multi-agent retrieval framework that dynamically orchestrates specialized agents over multiple reasoning iterations based on the demands of each query. The framework includes: (1) a retrieval agent for scalable retrieval over large video corpora, (2) a reasoning agent for zero-shot contextual temporal reasoning, and (3) a query reformulation agent for refining ambiguous queries and recovering performance for those that degrade over iterations. These agents are dynamically coordinated by an orchestration agent, which leverages intermediate feedback and reasoning outcomes to guide execution. We also introduce a novel communication mechanism that incorporates retrieval-performance memory and historical reasoning traces to improve coordination and decision-making. Experiments on three TRECVid benchmarks spanning eight years show that our framework achieves a twofold improvement over CLIP4Clip and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods by a large margin.

</details>


### [423] [Give Users the Wheel: Towards Promptable Recommendation Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18929)
*Fuyuan Lyu,Chenglin Luo,Qiyuan Zhang,Yupeng Hou,Haolun Wu,Xing Tang,Xue Liu,Jin L. C. Guo,Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DPR框架使传统序列推荐模型能够通过自然语言提示动态引导检索过程，同时保留协同过滤信号，解决了现有LLM集成方法在效率和精度上的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序列推荐模型无法适应显式用户意图（如自然语言提示），而现有LLM集成方法面临效率与精度的权衡：LLM作为推荐器牺牲了ID检索的效率和协同精度，重排序方法受限于底层模型的召回能力。

Method: 提出解耦可提示序列推荐（DPR）框架：1）融合模块对齐协同和语义信号；2）混合专家（MoE）架构解耦正负引导的梯度冲突；3）三阶段训练策略逐步对齐提示语义空间与协同空间。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，DPR在提示引导任务中显著优于最先进的基线方法，同时在标准序列推荐场景中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: DPR框架成功实现了可提示推荐能力，使传统序列推荐模型能够通过自然语言提示动态引导检索，同时保持协同过滤的优势，为推荐系统提供了更灵活的用户意图理解能力。

Abstract: Conventional sequential recommendation models have achieved remarkable success in mining implicit behavioral patterns. However, these architectures remain structurally blind to explicit user intent: they struggle to adapt when a user's immediate goal (e.g., expressed via a natural language prompt) deviates from their historical habits. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer the semantic reasoning to interpret such intent, existing integration paradigms force a dilemma: LLM-as-a-recommender paradigm sacrifices the efficiency and collaborative precision of ID-based retrieval, while Reranking methods are inherently bottlenecked by the recall capabilities of the underlying model. In this paper, we propose Decoupled Promptable Sequential Recommendation (DPR), a model-agnostic framework that empowers conventional sequential backbones to natively support Promptable Recommendation, the ability to dynamically steer the retrieval process using natural language without abandoning collaborative signals. DPR modulates the latent user representation directly within the retrieval space. To achieve this, we introduce a Fusion module to align the collaborative and semantic signals, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture that disentangles the conflicting gradients from positive and negative steering, and a three-stage training strategy that progressively aligns the semantic space of prompts with the collaborative space. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that DPR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in prompt-guided tasks while maintaining competitive performance in standard sequential recommendation scenarios.

</details>


### [424] [SIDEKICK: A Semantically Integrated Resource for Drug Effects, Indications, and Contraindications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19183)
*Mohammad Ashhad,Olga Mashkova,Ricardo Henao,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SIDEKICK是一个基于FDA药品标签构建的知识图谱，通过LLM提取和Graph RAG技术将药物适应症、禁忌症和不良反应标准化映射到HPO、MONDO和RxNorm本体，提升药物警戒和临床决策支持系统的语义推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有药物安全数据集过度依赖MedDRA等术语系统，限制了语义推理能力以及与语义网本体和知识图谱的互操作性，需要开发更语义集成的资源来支持药物警戒和临床决策。

Method: 基于大型语言模型提取和Graph-RAG本体映射的工作流程，处理超过50,000个FDA药品标签，将术语映射到人类表型本体、MONDO疾病本体和RxNorm，使用语义科学集成本体作为上层本体，以RDF格式序列化知识图谱。

Result: SIDEKICK在药物再利用的副作用相似性任务中优于SIDER和ONSIDES数据库，实现了语义集成的药物安全资源，支持自动化安全监测和基于表型的相似性分析。

Conclusion: SIDEKICK知识图谱通过标准化本体映射解决了现有药物安全数据的语义限制，显著提升了药物警戒系统的互操作性和药物再利用分析的准确性。

Abstract: Pharmacovigilance and clinical decision support systems utilize structured drug safety data to guide medical practice. However, existing datasets frequently depend on terminologies such as MedDRA, which limits their semantic reasoning capabilities and their interoperability with Semantic Web ontologies and knowledge graphs. To address this gap, we developed SIDEKICK, a knowledge graph that standardizes drug indications, contraindications, and adverse reactions from FDA Structured Product Labels.
